TL;DR: 本文提出了两项系统层面的贡献来提升小语言模型知识蒸馏（KD）训练的效率：一是验证离线蒸馏（缓存教师模型的top-K logits）与在线蒸馏在训练损失上几乎一致，但迭代速度提升约29%，单张H200 GPU吞吐量提升高达41%，并可将教师模型从显存中移除；二是引入融合的分块KL损失（fused, chunked KL loss），避免物化完整词表大小的logit张量，使峰值显存与序列长度成线性关系，从而在单张GPU上将上下文长度提升4倍（32,768 tokens）。实验还通过输出头单独基准测试验证了从4K到256K tokens的显存与迭代速度扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型因延迟、成本和本地部署限制常常是唯一选择，但很少从头训练，通常通过知识蒸馏从压缩模型中恢复而来。这个恢复步骤很大程度上决定最终质量，但训练成本高昂。因此作者希望系统性地研究如何让蒸馏训练更加高效。

Method: 作者提出两项系统贡献：(1) 验证离线KD（一次性缓存教师的top-K logits并让学生对缓存训练）能达到与在线蒸馏几乎相同的训练损失，同时将教师模型从显存中移除，每迭代快约29%，单GPU H200吞吐量提升高达41%；(2) 引入融合的分块KL损失，避免物化完整词表大小的logit张量，使峰值显存随序列长度线性增长，从而在单GPU上支持4倍上下文长度（32,768 tokens）。他们还设计了独立的输出头玩具基准来验证该损失核从4K到256K tokens的显存和迭代速度扩展性，并汇报了关于损失设计和序列打包的消融实验。

Result: 离线KD在训练损失上几乎等同于在线蒸馏，但速度更快（每迭代约快29%，吞吐量最高提升41%）；融合分块KL损失消除了词表大小的显存峰值，使上下文长度在单GPU上扩展4倍至32,768 tokens；输出头基准测试确认了损失核在4K至256K tokens范围内的显存与迭代速度扩展性；大量消融实验（数百次）在大规模修复和损失设计、序列打包方面变得可行。

Conclusion: 离线KD与在线蒸馏在质量上等效但显著更高效，结合融合的分块KL损失，蒸馏训练可在更低的显存占用和更长的上下文长度下高效进行，使得大规模修复和数百次消融实验变得经济可行。

Abstract: Small language models are often the only option for deployment under tight latency, cost, and on-premises constraints, but they are rarely trained from scratch: a compressed model is usually recovered through knowledge distillation (KD). This recovery step largely decides the final quality, yet it is expensive. We present a practitioner's study of how to make distillation training efficient, organised around two systems contributions. First, we show that offline KD (caching the teacher's top-$K$ logits once and training the student against the cache) matches online distillation at near-identical training loss while removing the teacher from memory, running about 29\% faster per iteration, and reaching up to 41\% higher throughput on a single H200 GPU. Second, we introduce a \emph{fused, chunked KL loss} that never materialises the full vocabulary-sized logit tensor, making peak memory linear in the sequence length. This removes the memory spike that otherwise caps context length and lets us train at four times the context (32{,}768 tokens) on a single GPU. A separate output-head-only toy benchmark isolates the loss kernel and confirms its memory and iteration-rate scaling from 4K to 256K tokens. Together these make large-scale healing and hundreds of ablations affordable. We also report supporting ablations on loss design and sequence packing. We release our chunked-loss implementation: https://github.com/CompactifAI/Full-Chunked-KL-Loss.

</details>


### [197] [M-GATE: Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03803)
*Tomáš Burkert,Angelika Peljak-Łapińska,David Zelený*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出M-GATE基准，用于评测多语言模型的真实语言能力（而非任务执行能力），涵盖30种类型多样的语言、三个任务（语法错误检测、往返翻译、分词器效率），发现流畅度与熟练度严重分离：最强模型在对抗性语法项上MCC仅0.36，翻译质量与预训练数据占比高度相关（r=0.86）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准混淆了"用语言执行任务的能力"与"对语言本身的掌握程度"，无法真正反映多语言模型的语言能力。论文旨在解耦流畅度（fluency）与熟练度（proficiency），提供一个更严格的语言能力评测框架，覆盖从高资源到低资源的30种语言。

Method: M-GATE包含三个任务：(1) 语法错误检测，基于语言学家手工构建的对抗性句子，聚焦难以掌握的语言特有现象；(2) 往返翻译，将共享英文源文本翻译成29种目标语言并返回，由三提供商LLM评审团评分（与专业标注者校准）；(3) 分词器效率辅助指标。评测了50多个模型、80多种配置。

Result: 流畅度与熟练度严重分离：翻译能力强的模型在对抗性语法项目上接近随机水平，最优MCC仅0.36；错误偏向漏报（接受不合语法文本）。翻译质量与预训练数据对数占比强相关（r=0.86），低资源惩罚显著但随模型迭代逐渐缩小。推理（reasoning）可靠地提升翻译质量，但对语法错误检测效果较弱甚至为负，最佳配置因任务而异。基准项持续更新并被私有化以抵御污染。

Conclusion: M-GATE揭示了多语言模型"能翻译但不懂语法"的现状，强调语言能力评测需区分任务熟练度与语言本身掌握度；同时指出低资源性能差距仍在但正在收敛，且模型配置（如推理）的最优选择依赖于具体任务，不能一刀切。

Abstract: Multilingual language models are deployed across a hundred or more languages, yet most benchmarks test whether a model can perform a task _in_ a language rather than whether it commands the language itself, conflating fluency with proficiency. We introduce M-GATE (Multilingual Grammar, Accuracy in Translation, and Efficiency), a benchmark of linguistic proficiency spanning 30 typologically diverse languages from high- to low-resource. M-GATE comprises three tasks: grammatical error detection on linguist-crafted, adversarially selected sentences that turn on hard, language-specific phenomena; round-trip translation of shared English sources across 29 target languages, scored by a three-provider LLM judge panel validated against professional annotators; and a supplementary tokenizer-efficiency measure. We evaluate over 50 models in more than 80 configurations. Fluency and proficiency come apart sharply: models that translate competently sit near chance on the adversarial grammar items, the best reaching a Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC) of only 0.36, and their errors lean systematically toward under-flagging, accepting ungrammatical text rather than raising false alarms. Translation quality closely tracks a language's share of pretraining data (r = 0.86 against log Common Crawl share), producing a steep low-resource penalty that is nonetheless narrowing with successive model releases. Enabling reasoning reliably improves translation, while its effect on error detection is smaller and for some models negative, so the best configuration is task-dependent. To resist contamination, test items are kept private behind a continuously updated public leaderboard, with illustrative examples released (https://m-gate.ai).

</details>


### [198] [VIBE: A VAD-Informed Benchmark for Entity-Centered Affective Profiling of Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03810)
*Andrei Chetvergov,Alexander Evseev,Timofei Sivoraksha,Stepan Ukolov,Mikhail Solovev,Danil Sazanakov,Sergey Bolovtsov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VIBE是一个面向大语言模型输出的实体中心情感画像基准，在Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD)空间中评估模型对政治人物、国家、宗教等社会敏感目标的情感倾向编码。其核心贡献是提出"测量契约"：将生成与外部评分分离、区分标量偏好/响应级VAD/目标定向VAD三种契约，并通过Affective Passport格式报告画像。三个实证层分别验证了效价可交叉验证但唤醒度与支配度存在方向性估计局限、整体响应与目标定向VAD是不同契约、提示协议漂移会影响画像，从而推动实体中心情感画像成为有记载的实践规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在描述社会敏感目标（政治人物、国家、宗教、组织、历史事件和社会群体）时，会在事实内容之外编码情感框架（如有利或威胁、平静或冲突、强大或脆弱）。现有工作在情感、偏好和情绪基准上各覆盖了该空间的一部分，但没有一个将目标定向的VAD归因、显式评分者契约和护照式报告格式结合起来。这导致对LLM输出的实体中心情感特征缺乏系统、可复现且可解释的度量方法。

Method: VIBE基准的核心是"测量契约"设计，包含三个层面：（1）测量构造分离——将生成过程与外部评分分离，并区分三种不同契约：标量偏好、响应级VAD（整个文本的情感基调）和目标定向VAD（文本中特定目标被呈现的方式）；（2）报告格式——通过Affective Passport记录评分者身份、覆盖范围、协议和解释限制；（3）三层实证验证——H1检验标量偏好是否涵盖唤醒度和支配度（通过效价交叉验证、人类标注者间一致性统计评估）；H2区分整体响应和目标定向VAD的差异；H3进行协议漂移诊断，考察提示条件变化对情感画像的影响。

Result: 实证结果支持测量契约的有效性。H1显示标量偏好不能涵盖唤醒度和支配度：效价发现可交叉验证（评分者-人类 rV=0.944，评分者间 rV=0.954），但唤醒度和支配度仅是单评分者的方向性估计而非点精确值（人类标注者间 rA=0.495，rD=0.702），与这两个维度已知的标注困难一致。H2证明整体响应级VAD与目标定向VAD是不同契约——同一文本可以整体携带一种情感基调，而对命名目标的呈现方式完全不同。H3揭示提示条件（协议漂移）会改变情感画像，因此每个情感报告都应附带上下文元数据。

Conclusion: 这些结果推动实体中心情感画像成为一种有记载的实践规范：模型输出的情感配置文件应随评分者身份、覆盖范围、协议条件和解释限制一同发布。VIBE通过将测量契约显式化，为LLM社会敏感目标情感评估提供了比传统单一情感/偏好打分更精细、更严谨且更可审计的基准框架。

Abstract: Large language models routinely describe socially salient targets, including political figures, countries, religions, organizations, historical events, and social groups, encoding affective framing alongside factual content: a target may appear favorable or threatening, calm or conflictual, powerful or vulnerable. Existing work captures parts of this space through sentiment, favorability, and emotion benchmarks, but none combines target-directed VAD attribution, an explicit scorer contract, and a passport reporting format. We introduce VIBE, a benchmark for entity-centered affective profiling of LLM outputs in Valence-Arousal-Dominance (VAD) space. Its core contribution is a measurement contract: VIBE separates generation from external scoring, distinguishes scalar favorability, response-level VAD, and target-directed VAD, and reports profiles through an Affective Passport. Three empirical layers support the contract. H1 shows scalar favorability does not subsume arousal and dominance: valence findings are cross-validated (rV = 0.944 judge-human, rV = 0.954 inter-scorer); arousal and dominance are single-scorer directional estimates, not point-precise, consistent with known inter-annotator difficulty on these axes (rA = 0.495, rD = 0.702 among human annotators). H2 shows whole-response and target-directed VAD are different contracts: the same text can carry one affective tone overall while representing the named target differently. H3 is a protocol-drift diagnostic: elicitation conditions shift profiles, motivating context metadata in every affective report. These results motivate entity-centered affective profiling as a documented practice: profiles should be released with scorer identity, coverage, protocol, and interpretation limits.

</details>


### [199] [Sensitivity, Causality, and Repair Dissociate: A Layer-Wise Analysis of Perturbation Robustness and Its Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03842)
*Nathan Labiosa,David Buff,Ena Nayak,Erica Donno*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在表面扰动输入（错别字、OCR噪声、同音词）下失败时，"哪一层负责"的三种操作化定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿性）并不一致，并发现两种传播机制（尖峰抑制型和晚期累积型）。因果性定位的层放适配器反而会破坏下游计算，造成诊断标记的位置是最差的适配器放置点，同时提供无训练LRD预筛和默认最深层放置等实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型在表面扰动的输入上失败时，理解"哪一层出问题"对模型诊断和修复至关重要。但"哪一层负责"有多种可能的定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿性），这些定义可能指向不同的层，而以往研究常混淆这些概念。本文旨在系统化区分这三种操作化定义，揭示它们之间的解离关系及背后的机制，并指导实际修复策略。

Method: 在五个模型面板（Phi-3.5、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5-7B）上进行层级别的三向分析：（1）敏感性——表示发散最大的位置；（2）因果性——恢复干净激活能恢复预测的位置；（3）补偿性——小型适配器能修复损伤的位置。进一步通过Qwen2.5家族（1.5B至14B）进行尺度缩放实验，并在固定harness下对四个模型（3.8-8B）在GSM8K思维链任务和多项选择控制任务上进行层扫描验证。

Result: 发现三种层映射彼此解离；五种模型呈现两种传播机制（尖峰抑制型与晚期累积型）；在通过80%身份修补门槛的两个模型上，敏感性与因果性呈反相关（rho=-0.72至-0.88）；Qwen2.5内部的缩放显示晚期累积特征随规模单调增强（另一家族验证一致）；级联破坏机制解释了解离现象——在因果性标记的早期层放适配器会破坏完好下游计算；在GSM8K推理链上被标记的层确为最有害的适配器窗口；多项选择对照中符号一致但效应强烈衰减。还发现表示稳定性损失在充足生成预算下收益反转——截断的思维链曾被误判为空——为思维链任务上的干预评估提供方法论警示。

Conclusion: 三层定义（敏感性、因果性、补偿性）的解离表明不能简单用单一"责任层"来诊断模型故障；因果性标记的层应避免放适配器，而应遵循"默认最深层"放置规则；缩放使晚期累积机制增强，提示更大模型可能对早期干预更不敏感；任何在思维链任务上评估干预的方法都需要提供充足的生成预算以避免截断带来的虚假收益。

Abstract: When a language model fails on surface-perturbed input (typos, OCR noise, homophones), "which layer is responsible" has three natural operationalizations: where representations diverge most (sensitivity), where restoring clean activations recovers the prediction (causality), and where a small adapter can repair the damage (compensatory capacity) - and we show these three layer maps dissociate. Across a five-model panel we identify two propagation regimes - spike-and-suppress (Phi-3.5, Gemma-2-9B) and late-accumulation (Llama-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5-7B) - and on the two models meeting an 80% identity-patch gate, sensitivity and causality are anti-correlated (rho = -0.72 to -0.88). Within-family scaling on Qwen2.5 (1.5B to 14B) shows the late-accumulation signature strengthening monotonically with scale, corroborated on a second family. We propose cascade disruption as the mechanism behind the dissociation: adapters placed at causally implicated early layers break intact downstream computation, making diagnostic-flagged sites the worst adapter placements. A fixed-harness layer sweep across four models (3.8-8B) confirms the core prediction on chain-of-thought GSM8K - the flagged sites are the most damaging adapter windows on every adjudicable model - and is sign-consistent but strongly attenuated on a multiple-choice control, consistent with damage that compounds with generation length. The sweep yields practical guidance: a training-free LRD pre-screen and a default-deepest placement rule, though absolute gains over no-adapter baselines remain small. Finally, apparent gains from a representation-stability loss reverse under an adequate generation budget - truncated chain-of-thought had been scored as empty - a methodological warning for any intervention evaluated on chain-of-thought tasks.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Representational Similarity: Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain for Generative Plagiarism Detection and Candidate Source Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03859)
*Peijia Guo,Wenxuan Xie,ZiGuang Li,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCDG（Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain），一种无训练、方向性的生成式剽窃检测框架，通过对比冻结语言模型在有/无候选源文本条件下对可疑文档的描述长度差异，在token级别量化源文本提供的增量预测证据，在PAN 2025和PAN 2026基准上超越所有基线，实现高精度源复用检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对学术诚信和同行评审构成挑战，但生成式剽窃检测仍是未被充分探索且基本未解决的难题。现有方法要么仅检测AI参与度（可能被允许）而非源复用，要么基于相似度的方法在大量改写和多源合成后失效。因此需要一种能够识别源文本复用（而非单纯AI参与）的稳健检测方法。

Method: SCDG基于概率预测的描述长度视角：相关辅助信息可以降低目标序列的编码长度。具体做法是冻结一个语言模型，分别计算在无条件情况下和以候选源文本S为条件下，对可疑文档P的描述长度（代码长度），两者的对比产生差异，即token级别的对数似然增益，衡量S提供的增量预测证据。该框架是方向性的（区分P源自S还是S源自P）、训练无关的（training-free），且可在token级别分解。

Result: 在PAN 2025导出的成对基准上，SCDG达到0.92 Precision、0.97 Recall和0.94 F1，超越所有基线；在PAN 2026多源检索任务上，达到0.83 nDCG@10和0.96 Recall@100，超越所有基线。在同主题、同事件的Multi-News测试中，校准后的增益分布SCDG分类器仅预测0.125%的文本对存在源复用，验证了评估协议下对主题重叠的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCDG被确立为一种统一且可在token级分解的信号，用于在广泛变换下检测特定源的文本内容复用，有效解决传统方法在大量改写和多源合成下的失效问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) pose challenges to academic integrity and peer review. Yet generative plagiarism detection remains an underexplored and largely unresolved challenge. Prior work on LLM-generated-text detection targets AI involvement, which may be permissible, rather than source reuse, while similarity-based methods struggle after extensive rewriting and multi-source synthesis. Motivated by the description-length view of probabilistic prediction, in which relevant side information can reduce a target sequence's code length, we introduce Source-Conditioned Description-Length Gain (SCDG), a directional, training-free framework that contrasts a frozen language model's description length of a suspicious document $P$ with and without a candidate source $S$. This contrast yields token-level log-likelihood gains that measure the incremental predictive evidence supplied by $S$. We evaluate SCDG on the PAN at CLEF benchmarks for generative plagiarism. On a PAN 2025-derived pairwise benchmark, SCDG achieves 0.92 Precision, 0.97 Recall, and 0.94 F1, outperforming all baselines; on PAN 2026's multi-source retrieval task, it reaches 0.83 nDCG@10 and 0.96 Recall@100, surpassing all baselines. On a same-topic, same-event Multi-News test, the calibrated gain-distribution SCDG classifier predicts source reuse for only $0.125\%$ of pairs, supporting robustness to topical overlap under this evaluation protocol. These results establish SCDG as a unified and token-decomposable signal for source-specific content reuse under extensive transformation.

</details>


### [201] [SciRet: A Compute-Aware Empirical Study of Retrieval and Reranking for Scientific RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03860)
*Kaysarul Anas Apurba,Md. Hasibul Hasan,Rofiqul Alam Shehab,Asab Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SciRet是一个面向科学问答（CORD-19数据集）的检索增强生成的计算感知实证研究，在三种语料规模（1K/5K/15K论文）下评估固定RAG流水线，发现混合检索比单稀疏或单稠密检索更稳健，但MS MARCO训练的cross-encoder重排序器在科学语料上反而降低精度，同时生成忠实度随语料规模增加而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG研究多在单一或较小语料规模上评估，缺乏对计算资源与语料规模变化的系统性理解。作者想探究在科学问答场景下，固定RAG流水线在不同语料规模下的检索与生成表现如何变化，以及跨域训练的组件（如cross-encoder）是否适用于科学领域。

Method: 固定科学RAG流水线由以下组件构成：句子窗口切块（sentence-window chunking）、BM25稀疏检索、BGE-M3稠密检索、倒数排名融合（RRF）、可选cross-encoder重排序、以及基于grounded证据的答案生成。在1,034/5,160/15,480个chunk三种规模下进行系统评估，并用RAGAS度量生成忠实度。检索评估采用基于混合系统派生的伪相关性标签。

Result: 在设定条件下：混合检索比纯稀疏或纯稠密检索更稳健，在1K和15K规模下Recall@10达到1.000；MS MARCO训练的cross-encoder重排序器在科学语料上降低了检索精度，说明领域不匹配的负面影响可能超过更强查询-段落交互带来的收益；生成忠实度（RAGAS）随语料规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: SciRet提供了科学问答RAG系统在计算资源与语料规模维度上的受控对比证据。混合检索是稳健选择，跨领域训练的组件需谨慎应用于科学场景。由于检索评估基于伪标签，结果应视为受控比较证据而非基准宣称。作者公开了代码、索引和评估输出以支持复现。

Abstract: We introduce SciRet, a compute-aware empirical study of retrieval-augmented generation for scientific question answering over CORD-19. Rather than proposing a new model, we evaluate a fixed scientific RAG pipeline across three corpus scales: 1,034 chunks (1K papers), 5,160 chunks (5K papers), and 15,480 chunks (15K papers). The pipeline combines sentence-window chunking, BM25, BGE-M3 dense retrieval, reciprocal rank fusion, optional cross-encoder reranking, and grounded answer generation. Across these settings, hybrid retrieval is more robust than either sparse-only or dense-only retrieval in our setting, reaching Recall@10 of 1.000 at 1K and 15K. In contrast, an MS MARCO-trained cross-encoder reranker reduces precision on the scientific corpus, suggesting that domain mismatch can outweigh the benefits of stronger query-passage interaction. Generation faithfulness measured with RAGAS increases with corpus scale in our setup. Retrieval evaluation uses pseudo-relevance labels derived from the hybrid system, so we treat the results as controlled comparative evidence rather than a benchmark claim. We release code, indexes, and evaluation outputs to support replication and follow-up studies.

</details>


### [202] [MultiGlobeQA: A Multilingual and Globally Diverse Benchmark for Geospatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03882)
*Martin Böckling,Elizaveta Nosova,Heiko Paulheim,Andreea Iana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiGlobeQA是一个大规模多语言地理空间推理基准，包含46,060个问答对，覆盖14种空间函数族和15种答案格式，基于三个知识图谱的执行结果作为真实标签，覆盖201个国家和地区并通过收入与人口密度分层采样，提供英语及其他16种高资源/低资源语言的平行问题。实验表明，LLMs在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现崩溃，拓扑关系与方向任务表现最佳；检索和工具使用虽有较大提升，但即使提供黄金事实，性能仍低于三分之二，证明计算是瓶颈而非知识访问。模型在低收入地区表现欠佳，且该差距在提供黄金事实时反而扩大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间推理基准存在不足：多为合成或小规模、主要单语、对地理覆盖控制有限，无法全面定位LLMs在空间计算上的失败模式。作者旨在构建一个大规模、多语言、地理覆盖可控的基准，以系统性地评估和诊断LLMs的地理空间推理能力。

Method: 构建MultiGlobeQA基准：46,060个问答对，覆盖14种空间函数族（包括网格索引、形状计算、拓扑关系、方向等）和15种答案格式；基于三个知识图谱的执行结果作为ground truth；通过收入与人口密度分层采样覆盖201个国家和地区；每个问题提供英语及16种额外语言（含高低资源语言）的平行翻译。在参数型（parametric）、推理型（reasoning）和智能体型（agentic）三种设置下评估LLMs。

Result: LLMs在网格索引和形状计算任务上表现崩溃，在拓扑关系和方向任务上表现最佳；检索和工具使用带来显著增益；但即使提供黄金事实，整体性能仍低于三分之二，表明计算是瓶颈而非知识访问；模型在低收入地区表现更差，且提供黄金事实时该区域差距反而扩大。

Conclusion: 地理空间推理的核心瓶颈在于LLMs的几何与拓扑计算能力，而非地理知识获取；现有模型的空间计算能力严重不足，特别是在低收入区域和计算密集型任务上。这为未来改进LLM空间推理能力（如增强计算模块或专用工具）指明了方向。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning, i.e., computing distances, containment, and other spatial relations over real-world entities, is central to navigation and logistics, yet large language models (LLMs) struggle with the required geometric and topological computation despite storing considerable geographic knowledge. Existing benchmarks localize these failures only partially: they are synthetic or smallscale, largely monolingual, and offer limited control over geographic coverage. We introduce MultiGlobeQA, a multilingual benchmark of 46,060 question-answer pairs spanning 14 spatial-function families and 15 answer formats, with execution-based ground truth over three knowledge graphs. It covers 201 countries and territories via income- and density-stratified sampling, with parallel questions in English and 16 additional high- and low-resource languages. Across parametric, reasoning, and agentic settings, LLMs collapse on tasks requiring grid indexing and shape computation, while topological relations and directions fare best. Retrieval and tool use yield considerable gains, yet performance plateaus below two thirds even when gold facts are supplied, indicating that computation, not access to knowledge, is the bottleneck. Models also underperform on low-income regions, a gap that gold facts widen rather than close.

</details>


### [203] [DS@GT-ARC at eRisk 2026 Task 3: Sparse, Semantic, and LLM Reranking for ADHD Symptom Sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03883)
*David Guecha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文描述了作者团队在eRisk 2026 Task 3（ADHD症状句子排序任务）中的提交方案。该任务要求系统对Reddit候选句子按与ASRS-v1.1中18种ADHD症状的相关性进行排序。由于该任务首次举办且未提供标注训练数据，作者采用零样本实验、人工验证以及无监督/弱引导检索引擎。系统结合BM25稀疏检索、面向自我参照症状报告的证据感知重打分、基于嵌入的重排序、查询原型扩展以及LLM重排序，采用分阶段检索设计（BM25大规模召回+语义/LLM精排）。实验结果显示LLM重排序器取得最佳官方成绩，其次为原型查询扩展方案，人工top-10分析与官方专家评分趋势一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADHD是一种常见但常被漏诊的神经发育障碍，社交媒体平台（如Reddit）上存在大量有关ADHD症状的自述内容。如何从这些非结构化文本中高效、准确地检索并排序与特定ADHD症状相关的句子，是自动化心理健康筛查和辅助诊断的重要环节。由于该任务（eRisk 2026 Task 3）是首次举办，没有可用的标注训练数据，因此探索在零样本、无监督条件下构建有效的检索与排序系统成为核心动机。

Method: 采用分阶段检索设计（staged retrieval pipeline）：（1）使用稀疏检索BM25从大规模Reddit文本中召回候选句子；（2）对自我参照症状报告进行证据感知重打分（evidence-aware rescoring）；（3）利用嵌入（embedding）进行语义重排序；（4）引入查询原型扩展（query-prototype expansion）以丰富查询表示；（5）基于LLM的重排序器对最终结果进行精排。所有提交系统均遵循BM25大规模召回+语义/LLM精排的级联架构。

Result: 在所有提交系统中，LLM重排序器取得了最强的官方得分，其次是原型查询扩展方案。人工top-10分析结果与官方专家评分趋势保持一致，验证了分阶段重排序方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 分阶段检索与重排序（staged reranking）是处理零样本症状句子排序任务的有前景方向。未来可在此基础上进一步扩展，例如增强查询原型构建策略和优化LLM重排序机制。

Abstract: This paper describes our submissions to eRisk 2026 Task 3, ADHD Symptom Sentence Ranking. The task requires systems to rank candidate Reddit sentences according to their relevance to each of the 18 symptoms in the Adult ADHD Self-Report Scale (ASRS-v1.1). Because no annotated training data were released for this first edition of the task, we relied on zero-shot experimentation, manual validation, and unsupervised or weakly guided retrieval pipelines. Our systems combine sparse BM25 retrieval, evidence-aware rescoring for self-referential symptom reports, embedding-based reranking, query-prototype expansion, and LLM-based reranking. All submitted systems follow a staged retrieval design in which BM25 retrieves candidates at scale and semantic or LLM rerankers refine the final rankings. Among our submissions, the LLM reranker achieved the strongest official scores, followed by the prototype query-expansion run. Our manual top-10 analysis aligned with the official expert scoring trend, suggesting that staged reranking is a promising direction for further development.

</details>


### [204] [ANNOTARES: A Dataset for Extracting Logical Structures from German Statutory Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03898)
*Ronja Schwarz,Jannik Strötgen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了德语法律文本中构成要件（Tatbestand）与法律后果（Rechtsfolge）的识别与分割任务，并发布了ANNOTARES数据集（包含三个不同法律法典的跨度级标注）。作者对多种方法进行了基准测试，包括基于规则的方法、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF以及基于Transformer和LLM的现代方法，结果表明BERT和LLM模型在法律语言复杂句法结构捕获方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本的结构化自动分析是法律技术的基石，但提取其逻辑组成部分（即构成要件与法律后果）仍然是一个重大挑战。现有工作缺乏对德语成文法中这一核心逻辑结构的系统性识别与分割研究，亟需一个标准化的数据集和基准来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 作者首先定义了法律条件（Tatbestand）和法律后果（Rechtsfolge）的识别与分割任务；其次构建了ANNOTARES数据集，涵盖三个不同法律法典的德语法律文本，带有跨度级标注；最后对多种架构进行基准测试，包括基于规则的方法、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM-CRF、以及BERT变体和基于LLM的现代方法。

Result: 实验结果证明BERT和基于LLM的模型在法律语言复杂句法结构的捕获上取得了最优性能。数据集同时支持领域内性能评估和跨法典泛化能力测试，覆盖三个不同的法律法典。

Conclusion: 作者发布了ANNOTARES数据集以推动自动化法律推理领域的进一步研究，并确认了深度预训练模型（特别是BERT和LLM）在德语法律文本逻辑结构分析中的领先优势。

Abstract: The automatic structural analysis of legal texts is a cornerstone of legal technology, yet the extraction of their logical components remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we introduce the task of identifying and segmenting legal conditions (Tatbestand) and legal consequences (Rechtsfolge) within German statutory texts. To support this task, we present ANNOTARES (Annotations of Tatbestand-Rechtsfolge Sequences), a novel dataset comprising German law texts with span-level annotations. Spanning three distinct legal codes, the dataset is designed to evaluate both domain-specific performance and cross-statute generalizability. We benchmark diverse architectural approaches: a rule-based baseline, CRFs, BiLSTMs, BiLSTM-CRF, and modern Transformer-based models, including BERT variants and LLM-based methods. Our results demonstrate that BERT and LLM-based models achieve superior performance in capturing the complex syntactic structures of legal language. We release our dataset to facilitate further research in automated legal reasoning.

</details>


### [205] [Logic Before Language: Pre-pretraining on Formal Derivations Fosters Skill Acquisition and Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03930)
*Jo-Ku Cheng,Nikolaos Aletras,Marco Valentino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出逻辑预训练（Logic-PPT）作为LM的初始化策略，利用形式推导为模型注入丰富的结构性和语言性先验。在1000亿token规模下，Logic-PPT显著加速语言技能习得，比标准初始化节省360亿token即可达到80%的准确率；机制上，形式推导诱导了低秩、谱聚类的表征空间结构，且该几何特性使模型在约33%稀疏度下仍能匹配密集基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预预训练任务（如Dyck语言和程序算法）依赖过于狭窄的原语，无法捕捉自然语言的表达能力；且先前研究局限于较小的token预算，对技能涌现和表征动态的洞察有限。因此需要一种更具原则性的初始化策略，能在大规模数据下更有效地促进自然语言习得。

Method: 提出逻辑预预训练（Logic-PPT）：利用形式推导（formal derivations）作为预训练数据，通过绑定变量、连接量词与关系依赖、以及跨长上下文组合谓词-参数结构等抽象机制，为LM注入结构偏置。在100B token规模下进行系统评估，并与标准初始化和替代预预训练基线进行比较。

Result: Logic-PPT在100B token规模下显著加速技能习得，以比标准初始化少360亿token即达到80%的语言任务准确率，并优于其他预预训练基线。机制分析表明形式推导诱导了持续的表征结构重组，具体表现为低秩、谱聚类的表征空间。这种内部几何结构使模型在约33%稀疏度下通过剪枝仍能匹配密集基线的性能。

Conclusion: 逻辑预预训练是一种有效的LM初始化策略，它通过形式推导赋予模型丰富的结构和语言偏置，不仅显著加速大规模语言技能习得，还诱导出有利于模型压缩的内在表征几何，为预训练策略的设计提供了新的原理性方向。

Abstract: Pre-pretraining language models (LMs) on symbolic data can accelerate and improve natural language acquisition. However, existing pre-pretraining tasks, such as Dyck and procedural algorithms, rely on narrow primitives that fail to capture the expressive capacity of natural language. Moreover, prior studies remain restricted to relatively small token budgets, offering limited insight into skill emergence and representational dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose logic pre-pretraining (Logic-PPT) as a principled initialization strategy, leveraging formal derivations to impart richer structural and linguistic biases. Formal derivations require abstract mechanisms that are central to natural language, simultaneously binding variables, connecting quantifiers and relational dependencies, and composing predicate-argument structures over long contexts. Scaling our evaluation to a 100B-token regime, logic pre-pretraining substantially accelerates skill acquisition in LMs, achieving 80\% accuracy on linguistic tasks with 36B fewer tokens than standard initialization, and outperforming alternative pre-pretraining baselines. Mechanistically, formal derivations induce persistent structural reorganization, distinctively characterized by a lower-rank, spectrally concentrated representation space. Crucially, we show that this internal geometry enables improved model compressibility via pruning, matching the dense baseline performance even at $\approx$33\% sparsity.

</details>


### [206] [HalluTruthQA-4K: A Fine-Grained Corpus and Annotation Process for Arabic Hallucination Detection and Truth Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03966)
*Salah Eddine Bekhouche,Abdessalam Bouchekif,Hichem Telli,Mohammed-En-Nadhir Zighem,Abdenour Hadid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HalluTruthQA-4K是一个扩展版阿拉伯语问答幻觉评测数据集，包含4,000个专家策划的问答实例，覆盖伊斯兰知识、历史、科学和地理四个知识密集型领域。数据集为每对问答提供参考答案和五个干扰项，幻觉回答还配有字符级错误跨度标注、人工解释和分层幻觉类型标注。该数据集作为HalluScoring 2026共享任务Track 2的官方数据集，可用于幻觉检测、跨度级错误定位、解释生成和事实性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿拉伯语幻觉资源通常对整个回答分配二元标签（有幻觉/无幻觉），但无法提供关于错误内容的具体位置、错误原因或正确事实答案的详细信息。这种粗粒度标注限制了模型幻觉检测和修正能力的深入研究。

Method: 研究者扩展原始HalluTruthQA语料至4,000个实例，包含1,643个幻觉回答和2,357个非幻觉回答，标注了1,843个错误跨度。构建流程包括问题选择、受控回答生成、候选构建、专家标注、独立验证、裁决和质量控制。每个实例包含阿拉伯语问题、模型生成回答、验证过的参考答案和五个干扰项。幻觉回答额外标注字符级错误跨度、人工解释和分层幻觉类型。

Result: HalluTruthQA-4K提供了4,000个专家标注的问答实例，其中包含1,843个注释错误跨度。数据集还记录了标注指南、分类体系、数据格式、标注者间一致性指标和语料统计信息。

Conclusion: HalluTruthQA-4K是一个可复用的阿拉伯语幻觉评测资源，支持幻觉检测、跨度级错误定位、解释生成、事实验证以及阿拉伯语语言模型事实可靠性的全面评估，填补了阿拉伯语幻觉评估粒度不足的空白。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent Arabic answers while introducing factual errors that are difficult to identify and verify. Existing Arabic hallucination resources often assign a binary label to an entire response, indicating whether it is hallucinated or non-hallucinated, but provide limited information about the exact erroneous content, the reason for the error, or the correct factual answer. We present HalluTruthQA-4K, an expanded version of the HalluTruthQA resource containing 4,000 expert-curated Arabic question-answering instances across four knowledge-intensive domains: Islamic knowledge, history, science, and geography. Serving as the official dataset for Track 2 of the HalluScoring 2026 shared task, HalluTruthQA-4K extends our original corpus to 4,000 instances. Each instance pairs an Arabic question with a model-generated response, a verified reference answer, and five plausible distractors. Hallucinated responses are additionally annotated with character-level erroneous spans, human-written explanations, and hierarchical hallucination types. The corpus contains 1,643 hallucinated and 2,357 non-hallucinated responses, with 1,843 annotated erroneous spans. We describe the resource construction and annotation methodology, including question selection, controlled answer generation, candidate construction, expert annotation, independent verification, adjudication, and quality control. We also document the annotation guidelines, taxonomy, data format, inter-annotator agreement, and corpus statistics. HalluTruthQA-4K provides a reusable resource for hallucination detection, span-level error localization, explanation generation, factual verification, and the broader evaluation of factual reliability in Arabic language models.

</details>


### [207] [string2string Studio: An Interactive, In-Browser Platform for String-to-String Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03984)
*Mirac Suzgun,James Zou,Stuart M. Shieber,Dan Jurafsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了string2string Studio，一个基于浏览器的交互式字符串分析平台，涵盖NLP、计算生物学和数字人文领域。系统包含六大模块（比对、距离、相似度、搜索、生成指标和BLAST同源搜索），运行在字符、词、token、行和残基等多个粒度上。算法基于C++编译为WebAssembly，核心操作在本地运行，无需安装或上传数据。界面展示结果的"证据"（比对、编辑路径、指标匹配、搜索命中和同源轨迹），使方法可检查、可调试、可比较。内部基准显示相比Python前身最高提速2500倍，比通用原生C比对器更快的局部/全局比对，在声明设置下与独立参考精确一致。BLAST搜索的客户端blastn路径在匹配参数下与NCBI BLAST+的排名和统计紧密吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字符串分析方法学工具在黑盒式地返回分数，缺乏对结果的置信度解释和中间过程的透明展示，用户难以调试、比较不同方法在共享输入上的表现。同时，许多工具需要安装、上传数据或依赖后端服务，存在部署复杂性和隐私/数据安全问题。作者旨在构建一个在浏览器中本地运行、快速、透明、可验证的字符串分析平台。

Method: 该平台采用C++核心算法编译为WebAssembly（WASM），实现六大模块：alignment（全局/局部比对）、distance（编辑距离等）、similarity（相似度指标）、search（搜索）、generation metrics（文本生成指标）、BLAST homology search（同源搜索）。系统在字符、词、token、行和残基多个粒度上操作，并提供方法结果的"evidence"视图，展示比对路径、编辑路径、指标匹配等具体依据。还包含策展展示和Learn模式作为可复用的教学演示。

Result: 内部基准测试表明：与Python前身相比实现了最高2500倍的加速；全局/局部比对速度快于通用原生C比对器；在声明的参数设置下与独立参考实现精确一致。BLAST同源搜索的客户端blastn路径在匹配参数配置下，其结果排名和统计与NCBI BLAST+高度一致。平台为开源项目，可在string2string.org获得。

Conclusion: string2string Studio验证了将高性能C++算法编译为WebAssembly并在浏览器内本地运行的可行性，在不牺牲性能和安全性的前提下提供了完全透明、可调试、可验证的字符串分析体验。该平台通过"证据"驱动的界面设计，显著提升了方法比较和教学的可用性，为字符串分析领域的工具开发确立了新的范式。

Abstract: We present string2string Studio, an interactive in-browser platform for string-to-string analysis across natural language processing, computational biology, and the digital humanities. The system integrates six main modules (alignment, distance, similarity, search, generation metrics, and BLAST homology search), operating at character, word, token, line, and residue levels. Its C++-based algorithms compile to WebAssembly, so core operations run locally by default without any installation or data upload. The interface reports scores with their "evidence" (alignments, edit paths, metric matches, search hits, and homology traces), making methods inspectable, debuggable, and comparable on shared inputs. Internal benchmarks show speedups of up to 2,500x over the Python predecessor, faster global/local alignment than a general-purpose native C aligner, and exact agreement with independent references under declared settings. For homology search, the scoped client-side blastn path closely matches NCBI BLAST+ rankings and statistics under matched parameters. A curated showcase and Learn mode present canonical algorithms and metrics as reusable demonstrations. string2string Studio is open-source and freely available at string2string.org.

</details>


### [208] [When Attention Goes Blind: Numerical Failure in ALiBi Positional Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03994)
*Christopher Schröder,Lukas Gienapp,Ferdinand Schlatt,Martin Potthast,Gerhard Heyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示ALiBi位置编码存在一个此前被忽视的失效模式：其线性偏置缩放在浮点精度下发生下溢，导致大量注意力权重归零，使受影响注意力头"部分失明"。作者分析该失效模式的特征与影响，提出四种缓解策略，并在148M参数解码器模型和SOTA预训练模型中进行验证，发现该问题显著损害令牌检索但仅轻微影响标准基准，最终给出具体的训练建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALiBi是一种广泛使用的位置编码方法，但其线性偏置缩放机制可能在浮点精度下产生下溢，导致注意力权重被置零。这一失效模式未被先前工作所识别，可能影响大量基于ALiBi的预训练模型的性能，尤其是在令牌检索任务上。作者旨在系统性地揭示、分析与缓解这一问题。

Method: 作者首先分析ALiBi线性偏置在浮点精度下发生下溢的机制和条件，随后在148M参数解码器模型上开展全面预训练实验，以将ALiBi失效的影响与长上下文退化效应进行解耦。同时，提出四种训练期缓解策略（包括log缩放距离等），并对单一策略及组合策略进行系统评估，还验证了该失效模式在SOTA ALiBi预训练模型中的实际存在。

Result: 实验发现：ALiBi的失效模式能够显著损害passkey之类的令牌检索任务，但对标准解码器基准的影响较小；在四种缓解策略中，log缩放距离在passkey检索任务上提供了最一致的改进；尽管存在该问题，默认ALiBi斜率在needle-in-a-haystack检索上仍然是令人惊讶的强基线。

Conclusion: 作者提供了基于实证结果的ALiBi模型训练具体建议，说明如何在使用ALiBi位置编码时规避浮点下溢导致的注意力"失明"问题，同时指出默认ALiBi斜率的鲁棒性，为实践者在检索能力与通用基准之间的权衡提供了指导。

Abstract: We identify a previously overlooked failure mode of ALiBi positional encoding: its linear bias scaling underflows floating-point precision, which zeroes out a large fraction of attention weights and renders the affected attention heads partially blind. We analyze this failure mode, characterize its impact, and examine four mitigation strategies. We further demonstrate its occurrence in state-of-the-art pretrained models based on ALiBi. Comprehensive pretraining experiments with 148M-parameter decoder models help us to disentangle its effects from out-of-context degradation. We find that ALiBi's failure mode can substantially impair token retrieval while having only a minor effect on standard decoder benchmarks. We propose four training-time mitigation strategies and evaluate them individually and in combinations, finding that log-scaled distances yield the most consistent improvements in passkey retrieval. Despite this problem, default ALiBi slopes remain a surprisingly strong baseline, particularly for needle-in-a-haystack retrieval. Based on these findings we provide concrete recommendations on how to train models with ALiBi.

</details>


### [209] [PAST-Bench: Benchmarking the Foundations of Recursive Self-Improvement in Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04003)
*Shuhan Xue,Zixin Ding,Yichen Shen,Yinjie Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Yuxin Chen,Mengdi Wang,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAST-Bench基准，用于系统测试个人AI智能体是否真的能从跨会话保留的经验中实现递归自我改进。基准覆盖26个场景、204个episode，通过开关保留经验的对照实验，发现改善真实存在但不均匀。基于此开发Hermes+，通过五阶段定向干预提升从经验中学习的增益与路径证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进要求智能体将积累的经验转化为更好的未来行为。个人AI智能体在会话间保留了偏好、任务历史、工具例程与技能，但保留的经验是否真正随时间改善其表现从未被系统测试过。缺乏能隔离"保留经验"这一变量的评估基准，阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 构建PAST-Bench基准：让每个智能体在匹配条件下（开启/关闭保留经验）运行有序的全新会话任务序列，覆盖记忆、程序性复用、信息收集与更新四类能力，共26个场景204个episode。同时评估后期任务增益以及增益是否遵循"保存-检索-更新"的预期路径。基于发现开发Hermes+，在智能体循环各阶段（保存、检索、更新等）加入五项定向干预。

Result: 在七种基础模型和四个智能体框架上，改善真实存在但不均匀。拥有相同总体增益的智能体在路径证据支持上差异显著。Hermes+提升了保留经验的平均增益，提供更清晰的路径证据，在需要替换过时状态的任务上效果最强，但效果仍依赖具体能力与模型。

Conclusion: PAST-Bench和Hermes+共同为研究持续智能体如何从"保留经验"进步到"系统性地借助经验改进"提供了评估与诊断基础。保留经验的增益是否由预期的保存-检索-更新路径支撑，是评估自我改进时不可忽视的关键维度。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires agents to turn accumulated experience into better future behavior. Personal AI agents offer a concrete setting for studying this capability because they retain preferences, task histories, tool routines, and learned skills across sessions. Yet whether retained experience actually improves them over time has not been systematically tested. We introduce PAST-Bench, a benchmark designed to isolate this question. Each agent runs through ordered sequences of fresh-session tasks under matched conditions that turn retained experience on and off. It spans 26 scenarios and 204 episodes across memory, procedural reuse, information gathering, and update. We report both later-task gains and whether those gains follow the intended save, retrieve, and update pathway. Across seven base models and four agent frameworks, improvement is real but uneven across capabilities. Agents with the same headline gain can differ markedly in whether that gain is supported by evidence of the intended pathway. Guided by these findings, we develop Hermes+, which extends Hermes with five targeted interventions across stages of the agent loop. Hermes+ raises the average gain from retained experience and provides clearer pathway evidence, with its strongest improvement on tasks requiring outdated state to be replaced, although the effect remains capability- and model-dependent. Together, PAST-Bench and Hermes+ provide an evaluation and diagnostic foundation for studying how persistent agents can progress from retaining experience to systematically improving through it. Code: https://github.com/Gen-Verse/PAST-Bench

</details>


### [210] [TurnSight: Turn-Level Hindsight Self-Distillation for Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04007)
*Changle Qu,Sunhao Dai,Hengyi Cai,Yuqi Zhou,Xinran Chen,Simon,Jun Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TurnSight是一个面向工具集成推理（TIR）的轮级（turn-level）事后自我蒸馏框架，通过执行条件事后视角构造多水平回看窗口，利用跨水平方向一致性筛选可靠监督信号，并将事后信号跨兄弟轨迹归一化后自适应调制RL优势值，从而解决现有方法在长时程TIR中细粒度信用分配不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在TIR中依赖轨迹级监督，导致长时程场景下细粒度信用分配受限；而现有on-policy自我蒸馏方法虽能提供密集信号，但其教师分支的privileged context通常来自真实答案或检索技能，无法反映智能体实际访问的状态；此外token级监督无法捕获工具交互的回合级结构。这些局限性亟需一种能直接从执行过程中导出密集且结构匹配的监督信号的新方法。

Method: TurnSight提出一种轮级事后自我蒸馏框架：(1) 直接从执行条件事后视角导出监督信号，而非依赖真实答案或检索技能；(2) 构造多个具有不同前瞻视野（lookahead horizons）的事后视图，通过跨水平方向一致性来筛选可靠监督；(3) 将筛选后的事后信号跨兄弟rollouts进行归一化处理，用于自适应调制RL优势值。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明TurnSight的有效性，证明其在工具集成推理任务中相比现有方法具有优势。

Conclusion: TurnSight通过引入执行条件的事后自我蒸馏与跨水平方向一致性筛选机制，成功解决了TIR中轨迹级监督粒度不足和自我蒸馏上下文不匹配的问题，为长时程工具集成推理提供了更有效的密集信号训练方案。

Abstract: Tool-Integrated Reasoning (TIR) enables LLMs to solve complex tasks through iterative tool interactions. However, existing reinforcement learning methods often rely on trajectory-level supervision, limiting fine-grained credit assignment in long-horizon TIR scenarios. On-policy self-distillation offers denser signals through teacher branches with privileged context, but existing approaches typically derive such context from ground-truth answers or retrieved skills, which may not reflect the states actually visited by the agent. Moreover, token-level supervision fails to capture the turn-level structure of tool interactions. To address this, we propose TurnSight, a turn-level hindsight self-distillation framework that derives supervision directly from execution-conditioned hindsight. It then constructs multiple hindsight views with different lookahead horizons and selects reliable supervision through cross-horizon directional agreement. Finally, the selected hindsight signal is normalized across sibling rollouts and used to adaptively modulate RL advantages while preserving their original optimization direction. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of TurnSight. Our codes are available at https://github.com/quchangle1/TurnSight.

</details>


### [211] [WorldCup Arena: Prospective, Leakage-Free Evaluation of Frontier LLMs on a Live Tournament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04008)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项前瞻性（非回顾性）的LLM预测能力基准测试：在2026年FIFA世界杯的39天里，六个前沿LLM在每次开球前被要求预测全部104场比赛、12个小组冠军及赛前冠军池，共记录了4,494个评分预测。由于提问时答案尚不存在，评估天然无泄漏。结果显示：六系统在比赛结果上平均63.9%，与押注博彩公司热门持平；系统间共识高于正确率，多数投票无增益；系统对平局和进球数预测不足，比分预测集中于单一原型结果；准确性取决于比赛悬殊程度而非信息丰富度（最势均力敌的比赛中准确性崩塌）；系统间区分度不高。全部数据与评分代码已作为基准发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测能力基准几乎都是回顾性的——事件已经发生、答案已在网上，评估必须防御记忆化污染。作者希望设计一种从结构上杜绝数据泄漏的评估方式：在事件发生前提问，使"答案不存在"成为评估的天然属性，从而真实检验LLM的前瞻预测能力。

Method: 在2026年FIFA世界杯39天赛期内，六个具备扩展推理和原生服务器端网络搜索的前沿LLM，在每场比赛开球前被逐一要求填写包含七个市场的预测卡片（覆盖全部104场比赛、12个小组冠军及赛前冠军池）。由于提问先于事件发生，评估在构造上无泄漏。共收集4,494个评分预测，并与博彩公司赔率、系统间一致性等基线进行对比分析。

Result: 六系统在比赛结果上平均63.9%准确率，与押注博彩公司热门基准持平（事实上它们通常就这么做）。系统间共识程度远高于正确率，导致多数投票无增益。系统对平局和总进球数投入不足，比分预测高度集中于单一原型结果。准确率与比赛悬殊度正相关而与信息丰富度无关：最势均力敌的比赛中准确率崩塌（尽管资料最丰富），而关于赛事整体的预测则表现良好。六个系统间区分度不大：排名首尾稳定、中间波动，差距始终很小。

Conclusion: 当前代际的前沿LLM在体育赛事前瞻预测任务上尚未展现出与押注博彩公司热门显著不同的能力，其预测行为存在系统性偏差（低估平局与进球、比分预测同质化），且准确率主要受比赛本身的可预测性支配而非模型知识深度。作者将全部数据与评分代码作为基准公开，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Benchmarks that measure the forecasting ability of large language models are almost always retrospective: the event has happened, the answer is somewhere on the Web, and the evaluation must defend itself against memorisation. We report the opposite design. Over the 39 days of the 2026 FIFA World Cup, six frontier LLMs -- all with extended thinking and native server-side web search -- were asked before every kickoff, one match at a time, to fill in a seven-market prediction card for all 104 matches, plus 12 group winners and a pre-tournament outright pool; no answer existed when the question was asked, so the evaluation is leakage-free by construction rather than by filtering, and the frozen archive holds 4,494 scored predictions. What the tournament establishes is a set of behaviours the six systems share. On match outcome they average 63.9%, level with backing the bookmaker's favourite -- which is in fact what they usually do. They agree with one another far more often than they are right, so a majority vote adds nothing. They under-commit to draws and to goals, and crowd their scoreline picks onto a single prototypical result. Accuracy tracks how lopsided a fixture is rather than how much is known about it: it collapses in the closest ties, where the dossiers are richest, while questions about the tournament as a whole are answered well. On this task the current generation of frontier systems is not sharply differentiated: the standings hold up at the top and the bottom across the run and churn in the middle, and the margins stay narrow throughout. The briefing dossiers, fixtures and official results are released as a benchmark, together with the scoring code.

</details>


### [212] [SocietyBench: Forecasting Counterfactual Social-World Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04009)
*Zhenran Wang,Zhonghan Bian,Jinsong Li,Zhangyang Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大规模语言模型（LLMs）及其智能体在真实社会事件理解与预测方面的能力缺乏评测的问题，本文提出SocietyBench基准——从五个平台的Web新闻和社交媒体帖子中构建日期索引时间线（区分事实层与舆论层），再将时间线中的每个截止日期转化为审计过的预测问题集。为防预训练记忆污染，采用三步处理将命名实体替换并平移日期，生成结构相同但无表面标签的反事实社会世界。在5个异构事件、125个预测点上，6个前沿LLM中最强者仅得75.0/100（锚点为50）。概率校准与时间精度两个轴可解耦；三个共享基座模型的智能体框架未能超越基线；模型无关启发式逊于所有LLM。单轴单事件差距达21.4分，论证了多事件评测的必要性。全部数据与代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM及其智能体的评测聚焦于"完成任务"能力（如修bug、操作浏览器/GUI），而对其理解和预测真实社会事件发展的互补性社交能力几乎未被度量。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出评测LLM对社会事件动态的理解与预测能力的基准。

Method: 引入SocietyBench：端到端基准，从五平台（Web新闻+社交媒体）采集事件相关信息，提炼为按日期索引的时间线，将事实事件层与公众舆论层分离；将每个截止日期转为审计过的预测问题集。评分采用两个正交的100分轴：概率校准与时间精度。关键反记忆污染设计：三步程序将命名实体替换、按per-event常数平移日期，构建结构相同但移除表面标签的反事实世界，防止模型根据预训练记忆直接匹配。

Result: 在5个异构事件、125个预测点（中英双语）上，6个前沿LLM中最强者仅达75.0/100（平凡锚点50）。两个评价轴可解耦：模型可校准强而时间弱，或反之。三个共享基座模型的智能体框架未能在该基座上有任何提升；两个无模型启发式均落后于所有LLM。单轴单事件差距可达21.4分，支持采用多事件评测的必要性。

Conclusion: 本工作揭示当前最强LLM在社会事件预测方面远未饱和，两个能力轴（概率校准与时间推理）需分别评测；现有智能体框架对基座模型并无增益；评测应覆盖多个异构事件以避免单事件主导的误导性结论。所有时间线、问题库、真值与评分代码已开源发布。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and the agents built on top of them, are now benchmarked heavily on whether they can finish a task -- fix a bug, drive a browser, operate a GUI. A complementary social ability, namely how well a model understands and forecasts the way real social events unfold, has barely been measured. We introduce SocietyBench, an end-to-end benchmark that takes a one-line event topic, collects Web news and social-media posts across five platforms, distills them into a date-indexed timeline that keeps factual events and a public-opinion layer separate, and then turns every cutoff date on that timeline into an audited bank of forecasting questions. Questions are scored on two orthogonal 100-point axes: probability calibration and temporal accuracy. Before any model sees a timeline, a three-phase procedure replaces every named entity and shifts every date by a per-event constant, turning a real arc into a counterfactual social world -- structurally identical to what happened, but stripped of the surface labels a model could match against pre-training memory. On five heterogeneous events and 125 prediction points in Chinese and English editions, the strongest of six frontier LLMs reaches only 75.0 out of 100, against a trivial anchor of 50. The two axes come apart: a model can be calibration-strong but time-weak, or the reverse. Three agent frameworks built on a shared base model fail to improve on that base, and two model-free heuristics trail every LLM. Per-event gaps reach 21.4 points on a single axis, which is our main argument for evaluating on several events rather than one. All anonymized timelines, question banks, ground truth, and scoring code are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ISEE（交互式语义丰富系统），针对LLM智能体在数据任务中因字段描述模糊或不完整而性能受限的问题，通过评分系统衡量描述质量、收集领域知识并与用户协作丰富数据语义，从而提升下游任务（如实体链接）的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地用于数据任务，但其性能高度依赖于数据语义的清晰度和完整性。然而，实际中许多字段描述模糊或不完整，因为关键上下文（如自定义字段的含义）源自用户的领域知识，很少被公开记录。这一差距限制了智能体在下游任务（如实体链接）中的表现。

Method: 提出ISEE（Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system）系统：给定数据字段描述，ISEE通过评分系统衡量其质量，收集领域知识，并与用户协作丰富语义。

Result: 通过用户研究、自动化用户模拟、定量评估和案例研究，证明ISEE显著降低了认知负荷，提高了描述质量，并增强了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: ISEE有效弥合了数据字段描述中语义模糊/不完整的鸿沟，通过交互式语义丰富机制，在减少用户认知负担的同时提升了描述质量和下游任务表现。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [214] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits），将功能逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题。采用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT），扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，在退化布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务。实验表明该架构能从零自组装功能电路，在永久性硬件故障周围快速重新路由逻辑；对于软错误，在远超训练条件的损伤规模下实现>99.99%的恢复精度；并在比训练时更宽的电路图上表现出泛化能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算系统的容错依赖静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统展现出自适应可塑性，能通过动态重组来维持功能。作者受此启发，希望将生物自组织原则引入数字硬件领域，实现无需人工干预的自适应容错机制。

Method: 将电路功能逻辑生成与维护形式化为图上的元学习问题。架构使用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路中布尔门的查找表（LUT）；扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式，使其在退化的布尔搜索空间中导航以计算任务为目标而非再生固定目标状态。在训练中学习从零自组装电路，并在推理时通过重新配置LUT来绕开硬件故障。

Result: 该架构能从零自组装功能电路；在永久性硬件故障下能快速重新路由逻辑；对软错误，在损伤规模远超训练条件的情况下实现>99.99%的恢复精度；在比训练时更宽的电路图上精度有所提升，表现出跨电路尺度的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将生物自组织原则与数字硬件实践相连接，证明了受生物启发的动态自适应容错机制可以超越传统静态容错策略，在硬件故障处理中实现高效、可扩展且具有泛化能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [215] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Meta-Persona锚定结合过滤温度缩放(FTS)"的新框架来缓解大语言模型中的"人工蜂巢思维"效应——即模型即使在高温度采样下也会收敛到狭窄、同质化的共识。该方法通过两阶段生成过程：首先让模型自选独特人格作为锚点，然后应用双重采样筛选（Top-p过滤保证语法有效性，接着用极端温度T≥4.0探索拓宽的概率分布）。在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上对~20B参数的开放权重模型进行评测，平均成对余弦相似度从~0.85降至~0.65，显著减少了语义收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在"人工蜂巢思维"效应，即使在高温度采样下，模型对开放性问题也会产生高度同质化的回答（内部回答间相似度高达0.80-0.90），这种语义坍塌限制了AI的多样性，使其无法达到人类级别的类型学多样性。

Method: 提出Meta-Persona Anchoring与Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)相结合的两阶段框架。第一阶段：提示模型自我选择独特的、个性化的persona来锚定生成起点；第二阶段：应用双重采样筛——先用Top-p过滤保证语法有效性，再对存活候选使用极端温度缩放(T≥4.0)探索拓宽的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上对~20B参数的SOTA开放权重模型进行评测，平均成对余弦相似度从~0.85降至~0.65，多数问题阈值降至0.7以下，显著缩小了人工模式坍塌与人类多样性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架有效缓解了LLM的语义收敛问题，大幅提升了生成多样性，并以开源框架形式提供实现，以支持更多样化和更具创造性的AI部署。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [216] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PULSE是一个受对象过程方法论启发的语言，将四个操作角色及其写效应本地化在一个类型化运行时中，通过契约固定证据非覆盖、分支隔离、多主体定时器、守卫状态变更和事件排序等安全属性。核心演算给出效应约束引理和六个安全性质，Lean 4验证内核类比实现。在冷链追踪、37,440条时序轨迹变异测试及NOAA IBTrACS数据集上验证了轨迹一致性和变异区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程往往将可接受状态、观测、约束、流程和假设场景分布在多个工件中，其组合执行契约仍停留在外部，缺乏一种能将操作角色和写效应局部化、且具备正式安全保证的统一运行时。

Method: 提出PULSE语言，受Object-Process-Methodology启发，用模式表示操作角色而非模态/道义逻辑。实现契约包含证据非覆盖、分支隔离、基于多主体的定时器、守卫状态变更、按声明排序的事件时空排序。外部运行器决定证据是否成为权威动作，GeoSPARQL/SOSA/SHACL作为生成视图。给出核心演算的效应约束引理和六个安全属性，用Lean 4验证内核类比，并通过88个测试、3,534个有界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时内核用例验证实现。

Result: 在37,440条生成的时序轨迹中，PULSE与独立工作流匹配并区分十个单字段变异体。在NOAA IBTrACS 1980以来的完整子集上，与GEOS和事件扫描在1,476,290个过渡区对上达成一致，包括4,800个采样和12,831个持续时间限定事件。两个独立实现复现了测试的冷链追踪。项目特定的GeoSPARQL探针测量接口覆盖率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在被测片段上，契约本地化、安全论证和轨迹一致性均得到支持；但语言优越性和可用性不在评估范围内，留待后续工作。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [217] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）对工具间关系进行显式建模，用于LLM代理的动态规划和执行，在AppWorld基准上显著提升任务完成率并降低API调用、LLM交互和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用代理通常依赖LLM从文本描述中隐式推断工具组合，导致在复杂任务中出现低效探索和不可靠执行；此外，真实世界执行环境不断演化，隐含推理难以适应。因此需要在模式层面对工具关系进行显式建模，以克服LLM隐式推理的局限性。

Method: 1) 构建有向工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph），工具表示为从输入模式节点到输出模式节点的超边；2) 给定任务时，提取任务相关的工具上下文图，指导构建模式感知的任务DAG（Task DAG）；3) 执行过程中，通过缺陷导向扩展（deficit-oriented expansion）动态构建状态条件化的工具支持图，识别未解析需求并依据当前代理状态检索支持性生产者工具来实现每个子任务。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，HyperAgent在提升任务完成性能的同时，相比现有代理基线减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: 通过在模式层面对工具关系进行图结构化的显式建模，能够实现更可靠的工具使用规划和高效执行，在真实世界复杂任务中优于依赖隐式推理的现有LLM代理方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [218] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了XAI（可解释人工智能）在欧盟"解释权"背景下的研究现状，审阅了57篇全文后发现仅19篇真正整合了法律与技术视角，并识别出三大问题模式：GDPR法律依据误认、对CJEU Dun & Bradstreet判决缺乏关注、以及解释形式与内容的混淆。文章提出Addressee/Purpose框架、四阶段实施方案和六个开放研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟法律赋予受影响个体对算法决策的"解释权"，但XAI能否在实践中满足这一权利尚不清楚。这不仅关乎学术问题，更直接影响个体挑战自动化决策的能力。随着《人工智能法案》（AIA）于2024年7月最终版发布（第86条后期添加），需要系统评估XAI研究在多大程度上真正回应了这一法律要求。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法：从2643条广泛搜索结果中筛选审阅57篇全文，重点考察2024年后的出版物（因AIA最终版于2024年7月发布）。通过法律和技术双重视角的整合程度对文献进行评估，识别跨学科综合的差距，并归纳出三个问题模式，进而提出Addressee/Purpose分析框架。

Result: 在57篇审阅文献中，仅19篇（约占三分之一）展示了法律与技术视角的实质性整合。发现了三个普遍问题：多数研究误认GDPR法律依据；很少涉及CJEU的Dun & Bradstreet判决（可能因发表时间滞后）；解释的形式（由接收者决定）与内容（由法律目的决定）经常被混淆。

Conclusion: 当前XAI研究在法律与技术交叉领域的综合仍显不足，导致"解释权"面临成为仅有形式义务而无技术实现路径的风险。论文提出的框架、实施方案和研究问题为弥合这一鸿沟提供了方向，但若缺乏进一步进展，欧盟的"解释权"可能沦为空泛的法律宣示。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework. We document three problematic patterns across the corpus: Most misidentify the GDPR legal basis; few engage with the CJEU's Dun & Bradstreet judgment (likely due to publication timing); and the distinction between explanation form (governed by addressee) and content (governed by legal purpose) is often conflated. We conceptualize this as the Addressee/Purpose Framework, propose a four-phase blueprint for operationalization, and identify six concrete open research questions. Without further progress, the Right to Explanation risks remaining a formal obligation without a technically realizable path to compliance.

</details>


### [219] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集合论（PST），一个从第一性原理出发重建认知架构的形式化生成框架。PST以最小操作集合（恒等函数传感器、集合论状态刷新、三类指称链）为基础，严格推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能，并宣称对罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈接地及电影剪辑理解等经典问题提供了新解法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测加工理论缺乏"预测"结构的操作性定义、预测错误的标准响应方式以及连续更新间一致性维持机制的规范说明；贝叶斯认知科学预设了封闭假设空间，且无法生成的解释概率分布对象如何成为离散可识别指称。两者均存在根本性概念空缺，亟需一个从底层重建认知架构的形式化框架。

Method: PST从三个最小操作出发：将传感器形式化为恒等函数，集合论状态刷新，以及三类基本指称链（指称、反指称、半指称）。在此基础上严格推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率与有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能。框架不模拟神经机制，而是作为任何在信息不完整与不可逆风险下维持内部一致性的系统的设计规范。

Result: 该框架为罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈的接地以及电影剪辑的理解提供了新颖的解决方案。本文的主要目的是通过公开学术记录确立PST框架的原创性与完整性。

Conclusion: PST提供了一种超越当前预测加工和贝叶斯认知科学局限的形式化生成框架，其价值在于作为设计规范而非神经机制模型，为认知架构的理论构建提供了新的公理化基础。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [220] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLAZE（Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation）范式，将AI从孤立研究任务的辅助工具重新定义为科学发现的组织基础设施。BLAZE通过连接持久知识、集体推理、实证验证和人类判断，构建一个连续的研究生命周期，使科学发现更具可追溯性、可重复性和累积性，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力和责任感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现经历了观测、理论、计算和数据驱动等阶段，当前面临的核心挑战不再是单纯产生更多信息，而是如何将不断增长的知识、推理和证据组织成一个连贯的发现过程。现有AI多被用于孤立的科研任务，缺乏系统性的组织架构来整合分散的科学活动。

Method: BLAZE提出"社会化科学智能"范式，将AI定位为科学发现的组织基础设施而非辅助工具。其核心架构包括四个相互连接的要素：持久知识库、集体推理机制、实证验证环节和人类判断，通过持续的研究生命周期将这些碎片化活动转化为累积性的探究、批判和修订过程。核心理念是科学智能并非仅由计算产生，而是源于知识、假设、实验和集体验证之间的持续互动。

Result: BLAZE通过组织人与机器共享科学过程，使发现活动更具可追溯性、可重复性和累积性，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力和责任感。社会化科学智能有潜力成为下一代科学的基础。

Conclusion: 社会化科学智能的目的不是取代人类的科学发现，而是扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度和连续性。BLAZE代表了一种从数据密集型科学向组织密集型科学的范式转变，为科学发现的下一时代提供可能的基础架构。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [221] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BAP-SQL将智能体文本转SQL中的观测生成视为预算控制阶段，通过估计查询风险、必要时重写SQL并将硬限制委托给独立运行时防护层，在有限预算下提升任务成功率，同时减少Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体文本转SQL中，智能体的行动决定后续观测，而宽泛查询会浪费上下文和数据库资源才出现有用证据。事后压缩无法恢复被省略的行或已耗费的工作，因此需要将观测形成本身纳入预算控制。

Method: BAP-SQL将观测形成作为预算控制阶段，包含三个组件：估计查询风险的模块、在有用时重写SQL的模块，以及将硬性限制委托给独立运行时防护层的机制。

Result: 在通用4B、FINER-SQL 4B和7B骨干上，BAP-SQL在紧预算下均提升成功率。在BIRD派生数据集上，相比匹配的SFT提升3.4/3.6个百分点，同时减少4.5/5.0%的Token使用。匹配的再训练和任务级迁移表明收益来自策略可见的规划和预算敏感的救援机制。

Conclusion: BAP-SQL的收益随模型能力和预算增加而衰减，在最宽松设置下出现反转，且不减少数据库工作量。这表明预算控制对观测形成的干预在有限资源条件下最有效。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [222] [VeriTrace: Human-Like Temporal Exploration Completes Agentic Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02878)
*Yu-Tung Liu,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriTrace是一种多智能体系统，通过引入"智能体时序探索"（Agentic Temporal Exploration）获得完整的调试动作空间，使Inspector智能体能够独立控制信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度，从而在VerilogEval-V2基准上首次实现100% Pass@1的功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM系统在自动化Verilog RTL生成上精度停滞在约95%，其根本原因在于调试动作空间不完整——系统限制了智能体可检查的信号、可查询的时间窗口或两者兼有，导致调试退化为在狭窄预定视图上的模式匹配，而非基于假设的根因分析。

Method: VeriTrace设计了一个Inspector智能体，运行在完整的调试动作空间之上，对信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度具有独立控制能力。这种被称为"智能体时序探索"的能力使智能体能够形成关于故障原因的假设、查询波形以获取证据、并迭代地完善理解，模仿人类验证工程师的探索性调试流程。

Result: VeriTrace在VerilogEval-V2基准上达到100% Pass@1，是该基准上首个实现完美功能正确性的系统。在共享Claude Sonnet 4.0骨干模型下，VeriTrace比最强复现基线高出5.1%，证明调试代理能力弥合了最终的精度差距。

Conclusion: 调试代理能力（debugging agency）是弥合大语言模型在硬件代码生成上最终精度差距的关键因素。通过赋予系统完整且独立的调试动作空间，使其能够进行假设驱动的根因分析，可以突破现有约95%的准确率上限，实现完美的功能正确性。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promise for automated Verilog RTL generation, yet state-of-the-art multi-agent systems plateau at ~95% accuracy on standard benchmarks. We trace this ceiling to an incomplete debugging action space: existing systems restrict which signals the agent can inspect, which time windows it can query, or both, reducing debugging to pattern matching on a narrow, predetermined view of circuit behavior rather than hypothesis-driven root-cause analysis. We present VeriTrace, a multi-agent system whose Inspector agent operates over a complete debugging action space, with independent control over signal selection, time-window bounds, and iteration depth. This capability, which we term Agentic Temporal Exploration, enables the agent to form hypotheses about failure causes, query the waveform for evidence, and refine its understanding iteratively, mirroring the exploratory process of human verification engineers. VeriTrace achieves 100\% Pass@1 on VerilogEval-V2, the first system to attain perfect functional correctness on this benchmark. On a shared Claude Sonnet 4.0 backbone, VeriTrace outperforms the strongest reproduced baseline by +5.1%, demonstrating that debugging agency closes the final accuracy gap.

</details>


### [223] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关、事后（post-hoc）的句子级归因解释器，用于解释仅通过封闭API访问的专有大型语言模型（LLM）。该方法训练一个能量基模型（EBM）作为代理，捕捉LLM在提示与响应之间的内部概念一致性，并通过该能量景观引导轻量级解释器网络的训练。该解释器是独立的工具，训练后无需额外API查询即可量化提示句子对指定目标输出的影响，从而解决专有LLM可解释性缺失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有LLM通过封闭API提供，用户无法获取模型内部参数或梯度信息，导致缺乏可解释性。这使得负责任部署面临关键挑战——无法理解模型为何产生特定输出，也难以进行有效的调试、审计和信任评估。现有的可解释性方法通常需要访问模型内部，不适用于此类闭源场景。

Method: 1) 训练一个能量基模型（EBM）作为代理模型，捕捉LLM在提示与响应之间的概念一致性，构建能量景观。2) 基于该能量景观，训练一个轻量级解释器网络，学习量化不同提示句子对目标输出的归因贡献。3) 解释器采用模型无关（model-agnostic）和事后（post-hoc）设计，训练完成后作为独立工具运行，无需再向LLM发送API查询。4) 通过全局训练（跨多样输入）而非单实例训练，捕捉更广泛的生成模式并缓解实例特定偏差。

Result: 实验表明，EBM能够准确模拟目标LLM的行为，解释器可以有效识别对生成特定目标输出最具影响力的提示句子。

Conclusion: 该方法为闭源LLM提供了一种实用的可解释性解决方案，通过代理EBM与轻量级解释器的组合，实现了在不依赖API查询的情况下对模型输出进行句子级归因解释，同时通过全局训练策略提升了归因的泛化性和稳定性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) accessed strictly through closed APIs has created a critical challenge for responsible deployment: a fundamental lack of interpretability. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic, post-hoc attribution interpreter operating at the sentence level. Our approach trains an Energy-Based Model (EBM) as a surrogate to capture the LLM's internal conceptual consistency between prompts and responses. This energy landscape guides the training of a lightweight interpreter network. Uniquely, our interpreter operates as a standalone tool; once trained, it quantifies the influence of prompt sentences on a user-specified target output without requiring further API queries to the LLM. By globally training a local interpreter across diverse inputs, our framework captures broader generation patterns and mitigates instance-specific biases. Experiments demonstrate that our EBM accurately simulates the target LLM, allowing the interpreter to effectively identify the prompt sentences most influential in generating specific target outputs.

</details>


### [224] [Hypercubes, Hyperplanes, and Constraint-Induced Complexity Collapse in Atomic Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02930)
*Irene Tsapara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过超立方体和超平面的几何视角重新审视高元原子概念学习，发现环境r维超立方体在逻辑复杂度上并非结构均匀：除全对角线外的每个超平面都崩塌为有限个初等等价类（界与项深度无关），而全对角线是例外且类计数无界增长。基于作者先前的高维框架，通过规范简单概念、最小序和代表性归约重新解释这些结果，建立了超平面行为分类学，表明复杂度是局部化的而非均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示原子概念学习中复杂度的真实分布规律。传统观点可能假设实例空间在逻辑上是均匀的，但本文通过几何观察指出这一假设不成立——超立方体的逻辑结构由超平面组织。理解这种非均匀性对高元原子概念的学习理论至关重要，因为它决定了复杂度集中在哪里、哪里是学习算法应该聚焦的区域。

Method: 采用几何-逻辑交叉的分析方法：1) 利用r维超立方体与超平面的几何结构刻画地面原子的逻辑复杂度；2) 借助作者先前开发的高维框架，通过规范简单概念（canonical simple concepts）、最小序（minimal orderings）和代表性归约（representative reductions）重新解释结果；3) 给出完整的二元案例分析、三元超立方体的显式处理，以及推动崩塌效应的归约机器的展开说明；4) 建立超平面行为的分类学，区分正交族、偏对角线和例外全对角线。

Result: 证明了除全对角线外的每个超平面均崩塌为有限个初等等价类，其界限不依赖项深度；全对角线是例外情形，其类计数随项深度无界增长。建立了超平面行为的高维分类学，表明复杂度是局部化的——集中在特定超平面（特别是全对角线）而非均匀分布在整个实例空间。三维情形已完整展现正交族、偏对角线和例外全对角线等本质现象。

Conclusion: 这一几何-逻辑视角澄清了原子概念学习中复杂度集中的位置，支持将概念学习理解为约束假设空间和结构化分类问题。全对角线作为唯一例外超平面的地位，暗示了复杂度增长的真正来源在于完全对称的结构，而非一般性的几何配置。

Abstract: We revisit higher-arity atomic concept learning through the geometry of hypercubes and hyperplanes of ground instances. Our starting point is the observation that the ambient r-dimensional hypercube of ground atoms is not structurally uniform. Its logical complexity is organized by hyperplanes: every hyperplane other than the full diagonal collapses into finitely many elementary-equivalence classes, with a bound independent of the term depth, while the full diagonal is exceptional and its class count grows without bound. This asymmetry is not merely geometric. It reflects the reduction-theoretic structure of the concepts themselves. Building on a higher-dimensional framework developed in the author's earlier work, we reinterpret these results through canonical simple concepts, minimal orderings, and representative reductions. This yields a taxonomy of hyperplane behavior in higher dimensions and shows that complexity is localized rather than spread uniformly through the instance space. The paper includes a fully worked binary case, an explicit treatment of the ternary hypercube, and an unpacked account of the reduction machinery that drives the collapse. The three-dimensional case already exhibits the essential phenomenon of orthogonal families, partial diagonals, and the exceptional full diagonal. This geometric-logical perspective clarifies where complexity is concentrated in atomic concept learning and suggests a modern interpretation in terms of constrained hypothesis spaces and structured classification.

</details>


### [225] [When Compression Scores Cannot Decide: Information Boundaries for Group-Robust LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02940)
*Andrew Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可复现的压缩统计量，尽管其裂半信度高达0.906并预测16.1%的提升，但所选端点在两个对照组上分别差6.0%和7.7%。作者通过"信息接口"框架建模这一差距，推导出等权组下的锥形定律（精确给出正线性固定候选损伤的池化代价），并构造三种两世界构型及精确观测纤维半径，以刻画池化矩、组局部矩与参考路径曲率未解决的歧义。实验表明：组解析对角方法在宽泛损伤排序上达到Spearman 0.9239，粗粒度深度分配在三个密集LLM上使最差组困惑度膨胀降低12.6%-20.9%，模型特定的完整掩码端点选择比参考方法提升2.7%-8.0%。在OLMoE中，路由器轨迹以114/192的准确率（对照最强重标记为81/192）预测单例方向，单层有限菜单决策在保留数据上实现最差组KL降低13.7%和7.2%。最后给出选择授权条件：需要完整候选端点或经验证的均匀性保证，并针对每个比较进行不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可复现压缩统计量虽然具有高信度并能预测性能增益，但在实际端点选择中可能选出劣于对照组的候选方案，说明统计量与最终选择目标之间存在系统性偏差。作者试图建立理论框架来解释并弥合"统计量预测"与"真实端点性能"之间的差距，从而澄清哪些区分能力被池化过程消灭，以及何时可以合理地基于局部测量进行选择。

Method: 论文提出"信息接口"概念来界定每种统计量可支撑的区分类型。关键理论贡献包括：(1) 等权组下的锥形定律，精确计算正线性固定候选损伤的池化代价（涵盖对角和满PSD二階矩）；(2) 三种两世界构型与观察纤维半径刻画池化矩、组局部矩和参考路径曲率未解决的结构歧义；(3) 组解析对角恢复方法用于群组损伤排序；(4) 粗粒度深度分配策略与模型特定完整掩码端点选择；(5) 基于路由器轨迹的单例方向预测及有限菜单决策框架。

Result: 组解析对角法在宽泛损伤序上达到Spearman 0.9239（细粒度序仍较弱）。粗粒度深度分配相比均衡均匀分配，在三个密集LLM上使最差组困惑度膨胀降低12.6%-20.9%。模型特定完整掩码端点选择较参考方案提升2.7%-8.0%。在OLMoE中，路由器轨迹预测单例方向准确率为114/192（最强重标记对照仅81/192）。单层有限菜单决策在保留集上实现最差组KL降低13.7%和7.2%。

Conclusion: 统计量的可复现性（信度）与选择正确性并不等同。基于局部测量构造候选是可行的，但选择必须由完整候选端点的评估或经验证的均匀保证来授权，且不确定性需针对每个比较进行校准。信息接口框架提供了量化池化损失的工具，使研究者能够判断何时局部信息足以支撑选择决策。

Abstract: A reproducible compression statistic can still select the wrong candidate. A dense pruning score with 0.906 split-half reliability predicted a 16.1% gain. Its selected endpoint was 6.0% and 7.7% worse than two controls. We model the gap through information interfaces that delimit which distinctions each statistic supports. For equal-weight groups, a conic law gives the exact pooling price for positive linear fixed-candidate damage, including diagonal and full PSD second moments. Three two-world constructions and an exact observation-fiber radius characterize what pooled moments, group-local moments, and reference-path curvature leave unresolved. A group-resolved diagonal recovers broad damage order (Spearman 0.9239) while fine order remains weak. Relative to balanced uniform allocation, a coarse depth allocation cuts worst-group perplexity inflation by 12.6--20.9% across three dense LLMs. Model-specific complete-mask endpoint selection improves over those references by 2.7--8.0%. In OLMoE, router traces predict singleton direction (114/192 versus 81/192 under the strongest relabeling). Finite-menu decisions on one layer yield held-out worst-group KL reductions of 13.7% and 7.2%. Local measurements construct candidates. Selection is licensed by complete candidate endpoints or a validated uniform guarantee, with uncertainty calibrated to every comparison.

</details>


### [226] [On the missing data layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02949)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataHub，一种面向拉丁美洲AI基础设施"数据集层"的任务优先数据基础设施，通过本体论结构 /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>/ 组织数据集，并包含数据集发现、元数据、贡献、许可和重用机制，以解决拉丁美洲AI数据集分散且总量不足的双重问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺少AI基础设施的两个基础层：数据集层和基准层。数据集层面临两个复合问题：发现（数据集分散在不同平台，缺乏共享索引）和供给（即使索引完美，数据总量仍远低于前沿AI开发需求）。

Method: 提出DataHub，一种任务优先的数据基础设施，通过本体论 /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>/ 组织数据，整合数据集发现、元数据管理、贡献机制、许可管理和重用机制。

Result: 论文提出了DataHub的概念框架设计，但目前仅停留在方案层面，尚未展示具体的实现或实证结果。

Conclusion: DataHub旨在通过任务优先的数据基础设施来解决拉丁美洲AI数据集层的发现与供给双重问题，为拉丁美洲AI生态建立缺失的数据集基础层。

Abstract: Latin America is missing two foundational layers of AI infrastructure: the dataset layer and the benchmark layer. This paper targets the dataset layer. The dataset layer faces two compounding problems: discovery and supply. Latin American AI datasets exist but are scattered across platforms with no shared index. Even with perfect indexing, the total volume would remain far below what frontier AI development requires. We propose DataHub: a task-first data infrastructure organized through the ontology /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>, with mechanisms for dataset discovery, metadata, contribution, licensing, and reuse.

</details>


### [227] [Neurosymbolic Reasoning with Incremental Knowledge for Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02993)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号分层强化学习框架InK，通过增量知识（InK）结合符号高层规划与低层神经运动原语，显著提升稀疏奖励长程导航任务的样本效率，并引入信念世界树搜索（BWTS）实现基于先验知识的最优符号规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦强化学习在稀疏奖励和长程推理环境中面临样本效率低下的严峻挑战。传统分层强化学习（HRL）将知识以固定、不可更新的形式编码，无法利用探索过程中逐步获得的增量知识进行推理，导致在环境知识积累不足时推理效率低下。

Method: 提出神经符号HRL框架InK：符号高层组件在可更新的增量知识表示上执行符号规划（如D*算法），低层目标条件化神经模块通过经验与奖励塑形学习运动原语。此外，为在具备世界先验知识时执行最优符号规划，开发了信念世界树搜索（Belief World Tree Search, BWTS）算法。

Result: 在导航任务上的实验表明，结合增量知识（InK）显著提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 通过在HRL中引入可更新的增量知识表示与符号规划，能够有效克服固定知识框架的局限，以更高的样本效率解决稀疏奖励的长程导航问题；BWTS进一步支持在先验知识下进行最优规划。代码已开源。

Abstract: (Flat) Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents face significant challenges in environments with sparse rewards that require long-horizon reasoning. A compelling approach to improve sample efficiency is to incorporate knowledge into learning and decision-making. In standard Hierarchical RL (HRL), knowledge is encoded in a fixed, non-updatable form, such as architectural choices, and remains unchanged throughout learning. With fixed HRL, reasoning with incremental knowledge learned during exploration is impractical before sufficient environmental knowledge is acquired, leading to poor sample efficiency. In this work, we propose neurosymbolic HRL with {\em Incremental Knowledge (InK)}: symbolic high-level components perform {\em symbolic planning} (e.g. using $D^*$) on an updatable representation of current InK, while low-level goal-conditioned neural modules learn motion primitives through experience using reward shaping. Experiments on navigation tasks demonstrate that incorporating InK substantially improves sample efficiency. Additionally, to perform {\em optimal} symbolic planning given {\em prior} knowledge about the world, we develop Belief World Tree Search. The code is available at https://github.com/CPS-research-group/ink_bwts.

</details>


### [228] [On the missing benchmarks layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02996)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 拉丁美洲缺乏AI本土开发的基础层——基准测试层。该层既能审计AI系统是否符合区域社会需求，也能引导AI在经济相关环境中进行优化。缺少这一层导致双重损失：缺乏可审计性和缺乏优化方向。论文提出EvalsHub，以LatamBoard为首个区域实例——一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，供大学、公共机构、专业社区和公司发布、执行、比较和维护跨模型、工作流和智能体的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺少AI基准测试层，导致公共机构无法独立评估外国AI系统，公司也无法优化AI系统以SOTA性能解决本地问题。这一缺失造成可审计性和优化方向的双重损失，而AI正日益成为关键基础设施。

Method: 提出EvalsHub——一个开放、任务优先的基准基础设施，以LatamBoard作为首个区域实例。设计原则为"一次构建，永久测量"，由机构在新AI系统发布时重新运行，行业团队在每次系统变更后重新运行。采用开放设计与激励机制驱动相结合的建设方式。

Result: 论文提出一个具体的架构方案（EvalsHub/LatamBoard），旨在解决拉丁美洲AI基准层的缺失问题，但目前尚未给出实证评估结果。

Conclusion: 拉丁美洲需要一个本土的AI基准测试基础设施，EvalsHub和LatamBoard提供了这一解决方案的蓝图。该基础设施需由多元利益相关方（大学、公共机构、专业社区、公司）共建共维，以实现AI系统的区域可审计性和本地化优化方向。
